# chatgpt response

ठीक है। अब **Module 13 — Team Lead Audit & Anti-Fraud System** बनाते हैं। यही वह layer है जो तुम्हारे management system को भरोसेमंद बनाएगी।

# Nivy Next — Team Lead Audit System

## 1. Basic principle

**Team Lead = performance owner, लेकिन performance data का sole source नहीं।**

मतलब:

> Team Lead report करेगा → system evidence देगा → QA sample check करेगा → Department Head approve करेगा.

---

# 2. Reporting Structure

```text
L1/L2 Employee
      ↓
Team Lead
      ↓
Department Head
      ↓
COO
      ↓
CEO
```

लेकिन data verification parallel चलेगा:

```text
                    ┌──→ Team Lead Report
Employee/System ────┤
                    └──→ Independent QA
                             ↓
                       Department Head
```

---

# 3. Team Lead के KPI

Team Lead को सिर्फ अपनी team का output नहीं मिलेगा।

उसके अपने KPIs भी होंगे:

| KPI | Example |
|---|---:|
| Team target achievement | ≥90% |
| Team quality | ≥95% |
| Reporting accuracy | ≥98% |
| Deadline adherence | ≥95% |
| Error escalation | 100% critical |
| Coaching completion | 100% |
| SOP compliance | ≥95% |

---

# 4. Data Verification System

मान लो Team Lead कहता है:

> “मेरी team ने 1,000 qualified leads बनाए।”

System automatically देखे:

**CRM records = 960**

फिर QA random sample:

**100 records**

में:

**92 valid**

तो verified quality:

**92%**

अब report:

> Claimed = 1,000  
> Recorded = 960  
> QA-valid = 92%

इससे तुरंत पता चलता है कि समस्या कहाँ है।

---

# 5. Three-Way Verification

हर important KPI में:

### Source 1 — Employee/System

Actual work.

### Source 2 — Team Lead

Management report.

### Source 3 — QA / Department Head

Independent sample.

अगर तीनों match:

🟢

अगर difference:

🟡

अगर major mismatch:

🔴

---

# 6. Random Audit

Team Lead को पता नहीं होना चाहिए कि कौन-से records audit होंगे।

उदाहरण:

Team ने:

**1,000 records**

दिए।

QA random:

**50–100**

check करता है।

### Sample में check:

- Correct?
- Complete?
- Duplicate?
- ICP match?
- Evidence?
- Timestamp?

---

# 7. High-Risk Metrics

इनका **100% system verification** जितना संभव हो उतना करो:

- Revenue
- Payments
- Client deliverables
- CRM stage changes
- Production automation
- Security/access
- Employee attendance where relevant
- Critical client communications

---

# 8. Low-Risk Metrics

इनका sampling sufficient हो सकता है:

- Research activity
- Content drafts
- Internal tasks
- Routine documentation

---

# 9. Anti-Manipulation Rules

### Rule 1

**Manual KPI entry minimum रखो।**

जहाँ संभव हो:

**API / CRM / n8n / database → dashboard**

---

### Rule 2

Completed task के साथ evidence:

**URL / record ID / timestamp / file**

---

### Rule 3

Completed status employee खुद बदल सकता है, लेकिन:

**approval अलग व्यक्ति करेगा।**

---

### Rule 4

Deleted/edited records का audit log रहे।

---

### Rule 5

Critical KPI में retrospective editing restricted हो।

---

# 10. Team Lead Daily Report

सिर्फ:

```text
TEAM:
TARGET:
ACTUAL:
QUALITY:
BLOCKERS:
RISKS:
ACTION:
```

### Example

```text
Lead Gen Team A
Target: 400
Actual: 385
QA Accuracy: 96%
Blocker: Data source issue
Action: Alternate source activated
```

बस।

---

# 11. Weekly Audit

Department Head हर week देखे:

| Metric | Claimed | System | QA | Status |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Leads | 1,000 | 980 | 96% | 🟢 |
| Outreach | 800 | 790 | 94% | 🟡 |
| Meetings | 40 | 38 | 100% | 🟢 |

---

# 12. अगर mismatch मिले?

### Small mismatch

**<5%**

→ coaching

### Medium

**5–10%**

→ investigation + corrective action

### Large

**>10%**

→ formal investigation

### Intentional manipulation

→ immediate escalation according to company policy.

**Accidental error और deliberate fraud को अलग treat करो।**

---

# 13. Team Lead Performance Score

| Metric | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Team KPI | 30% |
| Quality | 20% |
| Reporting accuracy | 20% |
| Delivery | 10% |
| Coaching | 10% |
| Process improvement | 10% |
| **Total** | **100%** |

इससे Team Lead सिर्फ target पूरा करने के लिए गलत data देने की कोशिश नहीं करेगा।

---

# 14. Surprise Audit

हर महीने कुछ random audits:

**Department Head + QA**

कर सकते हैं।

Team Lead को पहले exact sample नहीं बताया जाएगा।

लेकिन process transparent होना चाहिए—**surprise audit का उद्देश्य punishment नहीं, data integrity है।**

---

# 15. सबसे जरूरी बात

तुम्हें खुद Team Lead को check करने की जरूरत नहीं।

```text id="7h3y2c"
       ↓

       ↓

       ↓

       ↓

       ↓

```

**🟢 / 🟡 / 🔴 + evidence**

आएगा।

# chatgpt response

ठीक है। अब **Module 14 — Performance Management + PIP System** बनाते हैं। इसका उद्देश्य है कि किसी employee के खराब performance पर decision **emotion या personal opinion से नहीं, evidence से** हो।

# Nivy Next — Performance Management System

## 1. Performance के 4 Levels

| Level | Status | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 🟢 90–100 | Excellent | High performer |
| 🟢 80–89 | Good | Expected performance |
| 🟡 70–79 | Needs Improvement | Coaching चाहिए |
| 🔴 <70 | Poor | Formal intervention |

**लेकिन सिर्फ score देखकर termination नहीं।** Context और evidence भी देखना होगा।

---

# 2. Performance Problem आने पर Process

```text
KPI Miss
   ↓
Verify Data
   ↓
Root Cause
   ↓
Coaching
   ↓
Improvement Period
   ↓
Still Failing?
   ↓
PIP
   ↓
Final Review
   ↓
Improve → Continue
Fail → Further action
```

---

# 3. Step 1 — Data Verification

सबसे पहले check:

- KPI सही है?
- Data सही है?
- Target realistic था?
- Employee को required tools मिले?
- SOP available था?
- Training हुई?

**System की गलती को employee की performance problem मत बनाओ।**

---

# 4. Step 2 — Coaching

पहली बार problem:

### Manager conversation

> “तुम्हारा target X था, actual Y है। Gap Z है। चलो root cause identify करते हैं और अगले 7 दिनों का improvement plan बनाते हैं।”

Document:

- Problem
- Cause
- Action
- Deadline
- Expected result

---

# 5. Step 3 — Improvement Period

आमतौर पर:

**7–14 days**

का targeted improvement period रखा जा सकता है।

Example:

Current accuracy:

**82%**

Target:

**95%**

Action:

- Additional training
- SOP review
- Daily QA
- Smaller batches

---

# 6. अगर सुधार नहीं हुआ → PIP

## PIP = Performance Improvement Plan

PIP में होना चाहिए:

| Item | Example |
|---|---|
| Performance issue | Accuracy below target |
| Current | 82% |
| Required | ≥95% |
| Support | Training + QA |
| Deadline | 30 days |
| Review | Weekly |
| Owner | Team Lead |
| Final reviewer | Department Head |

---

# 7. 30-Day PIP Structure

### Week 1

**Training + controlled workload**

### Week 2

**Independent execution**

### Week 3

**Full workload**

### Week 4

**Final assessment**

---

# 8. PIP Evaluation

| Metric | Starting | Target | Final |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Accuracy | 82% | 95% | 96% |
| Productivity | 60% | 90% | 91% |
| SOP compliance | 85% | 98% | 98% |

अगर employee target तक पहुंचता है:

**PIP successfully completed.**

---

# 9. अगर PIP fail हो?

फिर तीन possibilities:

### A. Role mismatch

Employee को दूसरे suitable role में shift किया जा सकता है।

### B. Skill gap

अगर potential है → additional training.

### C. Persistent performance failure

Company policy, employment agreement और applicable law के अनुसार separation पर विचार किया जा सकता है।

---

# 10. Team Lead के लिए अलग rule

Team Lead की performance सिर्फ team output से नहीं।

Check:

**Team KPI + Reporting Accuracy + Team Attrition + Coaching + QA**

अगर team खराब है लेकिन Team Lead लगातार “सब ठीक है” report करता है:

यह **performance + management integrity issue** हो सकता है और अलग investigation चाहिए।

---

# 11. Performance Review Frequency

| Level | Review |
|---|---|
| L1 | Weekly |
| L2 | Weekly/biweekly |
| L3 | Monthly |
| L4 | Monthly |
| Department Head | Monthly |
| CEO | Quarterly |

Daily monitoring सिर्फ **exceptions** के लिए।

---

# 12. Performance Record

हर employee का छोटा digital profile:

```text id="o8l9nq"
Employee
Role
Level
Manager

Current KPI
Previous KPI
Quality
Attendance/reliability
Warnings/coaching
PIP status
Training completed
Promotion readiness
```

इससे HR और managers को historical context मिलेगा।

---

# 13. क्या नहीं करना है

❌ “मुझे लगता है तुम अच्छा काम नहीं कर रहे।”

इसके बजाय:

✅ “पिछले 4 weeks में target 95% था और verified average 82% था।”

❌ अचानक salary काटना।

❌ बिना documentation termination करना।

❌ Team Lead की एक report को absolute truth मानना।

❌ एक खराब दिन देखकर employee को खराब performer घोषित करना।

---

# 14. High Performer System

सिर्फ खराब लोगों का management मत करो।

अगर employee लगातार:

**90%+ KPI**

और strong ownership दिखाता है:

→ bigger responsibility

→ L2/L3 progression

→ bonus/incentive eligibility

→ leadership track

# Nivy Next — Compensation Structure

## 1. Level System

| Level | किस type का व्यक्ति | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| **L1** | Fresher / Junior | SOP follow करके execution |
| **L2** | Skilled Executive | Independent execution |
| **L3** | Senior / Team Lead | Ownership + team management |
| **L4** | Department Head | Department result |
| **L5** | Executive Leadership | Company-level responsibility |

# 2. Indicative Monthly Salary Bands

| Level | Monthly Fixed Range |
|---|---:|
| **L1** | ₹15k–₹25k |
| **L2** | ₹25k–₹45k |
| **L3** | ₹40k–₹70k |
| **L4** | ₹60k–₹1.25L |
| **L5** | ₹1L–₹2.5L+ |

ये broad planning bands हैं; specialized technical/sales roles इससे ऊपर जा सकते हैं।

---

# 3. L1 — Fresher

### Suitable Roles

- Lead Researcher
- n8n Operator
- Content Assistant
- Community Assistant
- SDR
- Admin Assistant
- QA Assistant

### Compensation

**Fixed salary + small performance component**

Example:

₹20,000 fixed

+ ₹2,000–₹5,000 performance incentive

लेकिन incentive **quality-adjusted** होना चाहिए।

---

# 4. L2 — Skilled Executive

### Suitable Roles

- n8n Developer
- SEO Specialist
- Senior SDR
- QA Coordinator
- HR Recruiter
- Content Executive

Example:

**₹35,000 fixed**

+ **₹5k–₹10k performance incentive**

---

# 5. L3 — Team Lead / Senior

### Suitable Roles

- Lead Gen Lead
- Delivery Manager
- Sales Closer
- Client Success
- Senior Automation Developer

Example:

**₹50,000 fixed**

+ **₹10k–₹20k variable**

Variable team/business KPI से जुड़ा होगा।

---

# 6. L4 — Department Head

### Suitable Roles

- COO
- CTO
- Sales Head
- Growth Head
- Operations Head

Example:

**₹80,000 fixed**

+ **₹20k–₹50k performance component**

यह role-level और business responsibility के अनुसार काफी बदल सकता है।

---

# 7. Sales Compensation

Sales में fixed + variable ज्यादा useful है।

### SDR

**₹20k–₹30k fixed**

+

qualified meetings / opportunities based incentive.

### Closer

**₹30k–₹60k fixed**

+

commission based on **collected revenue**, सिर्फ promised revenue पर नहीं।

### Sales Head

**₹60k–₹1.2L fixed**

+

team revenue/profit-based variable.

---

# 8. Incentive का Golden Rule

किसी employee को सिर्फ:

> “100 leads complete किए”

के लिए bonus मत दो।

क्योंकि वह quantity बढ़ाकर quality खराब कर सकता है।

Better:

**Quantity × Quality × Business Outcome**

Example:

```text id="t8f3qn"
100 leads
×
95% verified
×
ICP compliant
=
Eligible output
```

---

# 9. Team Lead Incentive

Team Lead का incentive:

**Individual output** से ज्यादा **team outcome** पर होना चाहिए।

Example:

| KPI | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Team target | 30% |
| Quality | 25% |
| Delivery | 15% |
| Reporting accuracy | 15% |
| Team development | 15% |

इससे Team Lead सिर्फ अपना काम करके नहीं बच सकता।

---

# 10. Department Head Variable

Department Head का variable company-level outcomes से linked होना चाहिए।

उदाहरण:

**Growth Head**

- Qualified pipeline
- CAC
- Conversion
- Content performance

**COO**

- Delivery
- Productivity
- Cost
- Quality
- Retention

**CTO**

- System reliability
- Automation ROI
- Security
- Development delivery

---

# 11. Incentive Guardrails

Bonus तभी मिलेगा जब:

### Condition 1

Minimum quality threshold pass हो।

### Condition 2

Major compliance/security violation नहीं।

### Condition 3

Data manipulation नहीं।

### Condition 4

Client-impacting critical failure unresolved नहीं।

---

# 12. Promotion

Promotion सिर्फ इस वजह से नहीं:

> “Employee को 1 साल हो गया।”

बल्कि:

**Performance + Skill + Ownership + Role readiness**

### Example

L1 → L2:

- 3+ months consistent performance
- KPI ≥90%
- SOP mastery
- Independent execution

L2 → L3:

- KPI ≥90%
- Complex work
- Mentoring
- Problem solving
- Leadership capability

L3 → L4:

- Department ownership
- Forecasting
- People management
- Business outcomes

---

# 13. Compensation Review

### Quarterly

Performance review.

### 6-month / Annual

Compensation review, company policy के अनुसार।

Salary increase के लिए:

**Performance + market benchmark + responsibility + business economics**

देखे जाएंगे।

---

# 14. तुम्हारे लिए सबसे important

शुरुआत में **हर role का maximum salary मत दो।**

पहले:

**L1/L2 → paid trial → performance → upgrade**

और senior strategic roles में:

**experienced hire / consultant / outsourced specialist**

use करना ज्यादा efficient हो सकता है।

# Nivy Next — Career Ladder

## 1. Universal Level Structure

| Level | Role Nature | Main Focus |
|---|---|---|

| **L2** | Executive | Independent execution |
| **L3** | Senior / Team Lead | Ownership + mentoring |
| **L4** | Department Head | Department results |

---

# 2. L1 → L2

Employee को यह साबित करना होगा:

| Requirement | Standard |
|---|---|
| KPI | लगातार ≥90% |
| Quality | ≥95% |
| SOP knowledge | Complete |
| Supervision | Minimal |
| Reliability | Consistent |
| Problem solving | Basic |
| Documentation | Good |

### मतलब:

**“मुझे हर छोटी चीज़ पूछनी नहीं पड़ती।”**

---

# 3. L2 → L3

अब सिर्फ अच्छा executor होना काफी नहीं।

Employee को:

- दूसरों को train करना
- complex tasks handle करना
- problems solve करना
- process improve करना
- junior employees review करना
- Team Lead responsibilities संभालना

आना चाहिए।

### KPI

**≥90% sustained performance**

और leadership capability।

---

# 4. L3 → L4

यह बड़ा jump है।

अब व्यक्ति को:

**tasks नहीं, department own करना है।**

उसे:

- Forecast करना
- Hiring plan बनाना
- Budget समझना
- Team structure बनाना
- KPI manage करना
- Cross-functional coordination
- Process improvement
- Escalations handle करना

आना चाहिए।

---

# 5. L4 → L5

यह executive leadership है।

Focus:

**Department → Business**

इस level पर:

- Revenue
- Profitability
- Strategy
- Risk
- Expansion
- Organization design
- Major decisions

important होंगे।

| Ownership | 15% |
| Problem solving | 10% |

**85+ + role requirements fulfilled**

# chatgpt response

ठीक है। अब **Module 17 — Hiring Pipeline System** बनाते हैं। इसका लक्ष्य है कि hiring तुम्हारे लिए एक बार-बार manually करने वाला काम न रहे, बल्कि एक **repeatable recruitment machine** बन जाए।

# Nivy Next — Hiring Pipeline

## 1. पूरा Hiring Flow

```text
Manpower Requirement
        ↓
Approved JD
        ↓
Job Posting
        ↓
Applications
        ↓
CV/Basic Screening
        ↓
Skill Test
        ↓
Shortlist
        ↓
Interview
        ↓
Paid Trial / Work Sample
        ↓
Final Evaluation
        ↓
Offer
        ↓
Background / Reference Checks where appropriate
        ↓
Onboarding
        ↓
30/60/90 Review
```

> **Trial में वास्तविक productive work कराया जा रहा हो तो उसे paid रखना बेहतर है और applicable employment laws/contract terms follow करने चाहिए।** Skill test को छोटा, standardized और evaluation-focused रखा जा सकता है।

---

# 2. Hiring Department Structure

शुरुआत में HR में बहुत सारे लोग नहीं चाहिए।

| Position | Level | शुरुआत में संख्या | काम |
|---|---|---:|---|
| HR/Recruitment Lead | L3 | 1 | Hiring system + approvals |
| Recruiter | L1/L2 | 1–2 | Candidates ढूँढना |
| HR/Recruitment Coordinator | L1 | 1 | Scheduling + records |
| Hiring Manager | L3/L4 | हर department से | Final skill evaluation |
| QA/Assessment Support | Shared | 1 | Tests verify करना |

**छोटी company में Recruiter + Coordinator एक ही व्यक्ति कर सकता है।**

---

# 3. Hiring Request

कोई department सीधे candidate hire नहीं करेगा।

पहले:

| Field | Example |
|---|---|
| Department | Lead Generation |
| Role | Lead Researcher |
| Level | L1 |
| Required Headcount | 3 |
| Reason | New client workload |
| Budget | ₹X |
| Start Date | X |
| Manager | Lead Gen Lead |

फिर:

**Department Head → COO → HR**

---

# 4. Job Description Structure

हर JD में:

### Position

**Lead Researcher — L1**

### Responsibilities

- Prospect research
- Company verification
- Decision-maker identification
- Data entry
- Duplicate checking
- SOP compliance

### Required Skills

- Basic internet research
- Spreadsheet knowledge
- Attention to detail
- Written communication
- Basic CRM understanding

### Nice-to-have

- Apollo/LinkedIn-type research tools
- CRM experience
- B2B research experience

### KPI

- Productivity
- Accuracy
- Data completeness
- SLA
- QA score

---

# 5. Application Screening

Recruiter हर candidate को manually पसंद/नापसंद नहीं करेगा।

एक scorecard होगा:

| Criteria | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Relevant experience | 20% |
| Required skills | 25% |
| Communication | 15% |
| Stability/reliability indicators | 10% |
| Portfolio/work evidence | 20% |
| Role fit | 10% |

**Minimum score → test**

---

# 6. Skill Test

हर role का अलग practical test होगा।

### Example — Lead Researcher

Candidate को:

**20 companies**

दिए जाएँ।

उसे:

- ICP identify करना
- Decision maker ढूँढना
- Required information भरना
- Duplicate check
- Sources/evidence देना

होगा।

### Evaluation

| Metric | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Accuracy | 40% |
| Completeness | 20% |
| Research quality | 20% |
| Speed | 10% |
| Formatting | 10% |

---

# 7. Interview

Interview का उद्देश्य CV दोबारा पढ़ना नहीं है।

### Round 1 — Recruiter

Check:

- Communication
- Availability
- Basic fit
- Compensation expectations
- Work setup

### Round 2 — Hiring Manager

Check:

- Technical/role skill
- Problem solving
- Decision making
- Real-world scenarios

### Round 3 — Senior/Department Head

सिर्फ important roles के लिए:

- Ownership
- Culture/work style
- Leadership
- Business understanding

---

# 8. Paid Trial

यह final practical validation है।

### L1/L2

लगभग **3–7 working days** का structured paid trial रखा जा सकता है।

### L3/L4

Role के हिसाब से:

**1–2 weeks** का project/work-sample assessment ज्यादा useful हो सकता है।

Trial में पहले से तय होना चाहिए:

- क्या काम मिलेगा
- कितने घंटे
- कौन supervise करेगा
- क्या KPI होगा
- quality threshold
- evaluation date
- payment terms

---

# 9. Trial Scorecard

| Factor | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Work quality | 30% |
| Productivity | 20% |
| Learning speed | 15% |
| SOP following | 10% |
| Communication | 10% |
| Reliability | 10% |
| Ownership | 5% |

### Decision

**85+ → Strong Hire**

**75–84 → Hire/manager review**

**65–74 → Hold/retest**

**<65 → Reject**

ये thresholds role के हिसाब से adjust किए जा सकते हैं।

---

# 10. Final Hiring Decision

Hiring manager सिर्फ:

> “Candidate अच्छा लगा।”

नहीं लिखेगा।

Final form:

```text
Candidate:
Role:
Level:

Test Score:
Interview Score:
Trial Score:

Strengths:
Weaknesses:
Risk:
Expected Performance:
Recommended Salary:

Decision:
HIRE / HOLD / REJECT
```

---

# 11. Offer Approval

Offer से पहले:

**HR → Department Head → COO**

और senior/high-cost positions में:

**CEO approval**

---

# 12. Recruitment Funnel Dashboard

तुम्हें यह funnel दिखाई देगा:

| Stage | Candidates |
|---|---:|
| Applications | 500 |
| Screened | 180 |
| Skill Test | 60 |
| Interview | 25 |
| Trial | 10 |
| Offers | 5 |
| Joined | 4 |

अब HR को पता चलेगा कि bottleneck कहाँ है।

|---|---|
| Qualified candidates | ATS |
| Time-to-shortlist | Timestamp |
| Interview show rate | Schedule data |
| Test completion | Assessment system |

**सिर्फ “कितने candidates भेजे” KPI नहीं होना चाहिए।**

# 14. Hiring Manager के KPI

Hiring manager की भी accountability:

- Interview turnaround
- Candidate evaluation quality
- Trial evaluation accuracy
- New hire 30/60/90 performance

इससे HR को खराब candidates देने का blame game कम होगा।

# 15. Candidate Database

हर candidate का record:

```text

 ↓
Role
 ↓
Level
 ↓
Source
```

# 16. सबसे important automation

जहाँ संभव हो:

automate करो।

पर भरोसा करोगे।

|---|---|---:|---|
| Vendor/Procurement Manager | L3 | 1 | Vendor ownership |
| Vendor Coordinator | L1/L2 | 1 | Communication + records |
| QA | Shared | Existing QA | Output checking |
| Department Owner | L3/L4 | Shared | Technical evaluation |
| Finance | Shared | Existing | Invoice/payment |

मतलब शुरुआत में **2 dedicated लोग + shared teams** काफी हो सकते हैं।

---

# 3. Vendor Categories

Vendors को अलग-अलग buckets में रखो:

| Category | Example |
|---|---|
| Automation | n8n / development |
| Design | Graphics/UI |
| Content | Writing/video |
| Lead Generation | Research |
| Marketing | SEO/ads |
| Development | Web/software |
| Operations | Back-office |
| Specialist | Legal/accounting/etc. |

---

# 4. Vendor Onboarding

Vendor से पहले:

- Company/profile
- Services
- Portfolio
- Team size
- Relevant experience
- Capacity
- Turnaround
- Pricing
- References where appropriate
- Security capability
- Communication process

लेना चाहिए।

---

# 5. Vendor Test

बड़ा project सीधे मत दो।

पहले:

**Small paid pilot**

कराओ।

Example:

अगर vendor 1,000 leads देने वाला है:

पहले:

**50–100 leads**

का pilot।

फिर QA।

अगर quality अच्छी:

**500**

फिर:

**1,000+**

---

# 6. Vendor Scorecard

| Metric | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Quality | 30% |
| On-time delivery | 20% |
| Price/value | 15% |
| Communication | 10% |
| Scalability | 10% |
| Reliability | 10% |
| Documentation | 5% |

---

# 7. Vendor Levels

### 🟢 Tier A

Trusted vendor.

- High quality
- Reliable
- Can handle important work

### 🟡 Tier B

Approved but monitored.

### 🔴 Tier C

Pilot/conditional.

### ⚫ Blacklisted

Serious breach/fraud/repeated failure के बाद, company policy के अनुसार।

---

# 8. Work Allocation

Vendor को सिर्फ:

> “यह काम कर दो।”

मत बोलो।

उसे standardized brief मिलेगा:

```text id="xqzj7y"
Project:
Objective:
Scope:
Deliverables:
Input:
Output format:
Deadline:
Quality standard:
SOP:
Communication channel:
Acceptance criteria:
```

---

# 9. Vendor QA

Vendor कहता है:

**1,000 tasks complete.**

System:

**Delivered → QA sample → Pass/Fail**

उदाहरण:

1,000 delivered

100 sampled

94 pass

→ **94% QA score**

फिर payment/acceptance policy के अनुसार।

---

# 10. Vendor Payment

Payment केवल invoice देखकर नहीं।

Flow:

```text id="3q4b3h"
Work Delivered
↓
Quantity Verified
↓
Quality Verified
↓
Acceptance
↓
Invoice
↓
Finance Approval
↓
Payment
```

---

# 11. Vendor KPI

हर vendor का dashboard:

| KPI | Actual |
|---|---:|
| Quality | 96% |
| On-time | 98% |
| Rework | 3% |
| Response SLA | 95% |
| Cost variance | 2% |
| Overall score | 94% |

---

# 12. Monthly Vendor Review

### Score ≥90

**Preferred vendor**

### 80–89

**Continue**

### 70–79

**Corrective action**

### <70

**Review / replace**

Thresholds service risk के हिसाब से adjust होंगे।

---

# 13. Vendor Risk Control

कभी भी:

**एक ही vendor पर 100% dependency**

मत रखो, खासकर critical process में।

Example:

```text
Primary Vendor → 70%
Backup Vendor → 30%
```

अगर primary fail हो जाए तो दूसरा तुरंत activate हो सके।

---

# 14. Access Control

Vendor को:

**Only required access**

दो।

उसे पूरे:

- CRM
- Client database
- Password vault
- Finance
- Internal documents

का access नहीं मिलना चाहिए अगर काम के लिए जरूरत नहीं है।

---

# 15. Client Confidentiality

जहाँ applicable हो:

- NDA
- Data-processing terms
- IP ownership
- Confidentiality
- Security requirements
- Access restrictions

contract में define करो।

---

# 16. Vendor Replacement

अगर vendor लगातार fail करे:

```text id="gq6d5q"
Problem
↓
Evidence
↓
Corrective Action
↓
Improvement Period
↓
Still failing?
↓
Backup Vendor
↓
Transition
↓
Offboarding
```

---

# 17. Vendor Manager KPI

Vendor Manager को:

- Vendor quality
- Cost
- Delivery
- Vendor availability
- Replacement readiness
- Contract compliance

पर judge करो।

सिर्फ “कितने vendors manage किए” नहीं।

---

```text id="v3j0s4"
                    COO
                     │

     Vendors       Vendors       Vendors
```

# Nivy Next — Client Delivery System

## 1. पूरा Client Lifecycle

```text id="clientflow1"
Sales Closes Deal
      ↓
Contract + Payment
      ↓
Client Onboarding
      ↓
Requirements
      ↓
Master Funnel / Project Plan
      ↓
Internal Assignment
      ↓
Execution / Outsourcing
      ↓
QA
      ↓
Client Delivery
      ↓
Feedback
      ↓
Revision
      ↓
Final Acceptance
      ↓
Reporting
      ↓
Renewal / Upsell
```

# 2. Client Delivery Department

शुरुआत में:

| Position | Level | संख्या | मुख्य काम |
|---|---|---:|---|
| **Client Delivery Head** | L4 | 1 | पूरी delivery |
| **Project/Account Manager** | L3 | 1–2 | Client + project coordination |
| **Project Coordinator** | L1/L2 | 1 | Tasks, deadlines, records |
| **QA Executive** | L1/L2 | Shared | Output verification |
| **Execution Specialists** | L1–L3 | आवश्यकता अनुसार | Actual work |
| **Vendor Manager** | L3 | Shared | Outsourced work |

**शुरुआत में Delivery Head + PM + Coordinator की roles combine भी की जा सकती हैं।**

---

# 3. Sales से Delivery Handoff

Salesperson deal close करने के बाद सीधे execution team को random message नहीं करेगा।

एक standardized **Handoff Form** बनेगा।

| Field | Required |
|---|---|
| Client | ✅ |
| Contract | ✅ |
| Package | ✅ |
| Scope | ✅ |
| Deliverables | ✅ |
| Timeline | ✅ |
| Pricing | ✅ |
| Client goals | ✅ |
| ICP | अगर relevant |
| Access required | ✅ |
| Communication preference | ✅ |
| Success metrics | ✅ |
| Special requirements | ✅ |
| Exclusions | ✅ |

**Missing information = handoff incomplete.**

---

# 4. Client Onboarding

Client को onboarding sequence मिलेगा:

### Step 1
Welcome + introduction

### Step 2
Kickoff meeting

### Step 3
Requirements collection

### Step 4
Access collection

### Step 5
Goals + KPIs confirm

### Step 6
Timeline confirm

### Step 7
First milestone

---

# 5. Master Project Plan

हर client का एक master project board होगा।

| Stage | Owner | Deadline | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Requirements | PM | Day 2 | 🟢 |
| Strategy | Strategist | Day 5 | 🟢 |
| Build | Execution | Day 12 | 🟡 |
| QA | QA | Day 14 | — |
| Delivery | PM | Day 15 | — |

इससे client project का पूरा status एक जगह रहेगा।

---

# 6. Execution Workflow

हर task:

```text id="execflow2"
Task Created
↓
Assigned
↓
Brief
↓
Execution
↓
Self-check
↓
QA
↓
Approved?
 ├── No → Rework
 └── Yes
      ↓
Client Delivery
```

**Execution → सीधे Client नहीं।**

पहले QA।

---

# 7. QA Gate

QA check करेगा:

### Scope

क्या जो बेचने का promise था वही बना?

### Quality

क्या output standard के अनुसार है?

### Accuracy

Data/technical/content errors?

### Branding

Client guidelines follow?

### Functionality

अगर automation/website/software है तो working है?

### Documentation

क्या handover information complete है?

---

# 8. Client Delivery

PM client को:

- Deliverable
- Summary
- What was done
- What remains
- Client action required
- Next milestone

देगा।

इससे client को सिर्फ file dump नहीं मिलेगा।

---

# 9. Revision Management

Client की हर request:

**Feedback → Ticket/Task → Scope Check**

फिर:

### In Scope

→ normal revision

### Out of Scope

→ Change Request

और आवश्यकता हो तो:

**Additional quote / timeline adjustment**

---

# 10. Client Communication

Client के लिए **एक primary owner** होना चाहिए।

Client को अलग-अलग:

❌ Developer

❌ Designer

❌ Vendor

❌ Researcher

से directly coordinate नहीं करना चाहिए, जब तक specifically जरूरी न हो।

**Client → Account/Project Manager → Internal Teams**

---

एक standardized report:

```text id="report7"
Completed:
• X
• Y

In Progress:
• A
• B

Results:
• KPI 1
• KPI 2

Issues:
• None / X

Next Week:
• A
• B

Client Input Needed:
• X
```

# 12. Client Health Score

हर client को:

🟢 Healthy

🟡 Attention

🔴 At Risk

दिया जा सकता है।

### Factors

| Factor | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Delivery | 25% |
| Results | 25% |
| Communication | 15% |
| Satisfaction | 15% |
| Payment | 10% |
| Engagement | 10% |

# 13. Escalation System

### Level 1

PM solve करेगा।

### Level 2

Delivery Head।

### Level 3

COO।

### Level 4

CEO — केवल major commercial/reputation issue।

इससे छोटी problem CEO तक नहीं आएगी।

# 14. Client Renewal

Contract खत्म होने से पहले:

**30–45 days prior**, जहाँ contract cycle ऐसा allow करे:

PM + Sales/Account team review करे:

- Results
- ROI
- Completed work
- Remaining opportunity
- Next phase
- Renewal proposal

# 15. Upsell

Upsell सिर्फ sales target के लिए नहीं।

पहले identify:

**Client की next business problem**

फिर:

**Existing result → New opportunity → Relevant service**

उदाहरण:

Client ने funnel बनवाया।

अगला logical need:

**automation + analytics + optimization**

तो relevant proposal।

# chatgpt response

ठीक है। अब **Module 20 — Company Policies & Operating Rules** बनाते हैं। इसे इतना practical रखेंगे कि employee को हर छोटी बात पूछने की जरूरत न पड़े और managers मनमाने तरीके से rules न बदलें।

# Nivy Next — Company Policy Framework

## 1. Policy Structure

हर policy में:

| Field | क्या होगा |
|---|---|
| Policy ID | Unique number |
| Policy Name | Policy का नाम |
| Purpose | क्यों है |
| Applies To | किन लोगों पर |
| Rule | Actual नियम |
| Exception | किन परिस्थितियों में exception |
| Approval | किसकी अनुमति |
| Violation | क्या होगा |
| Owner | कौन maintain करेगा |
| Version | Current version |

---

# 2. Attendance & Working Hours

| Rule | Framework |
|---|---|
| Working hours | Role/contract के अनुसार |
| Attendance | Approved system |
| Late arrival | Defined policy |
| Absence | Manager + HR |
| Unplanned absence | Immediate notification |
| Remote work | Approved arrangement |
| Overtime | Prior approval |

**Important:** local labour laws और employment contracts के अनुसार final policy बनानी होगी।

---

# 3. Leave Policy

Leave request:

```text id="leave1"
Employee
 ↓
Manager
 ↓
HR record
 ↓
Approved / Rejected
```

Emergency leave में:

**पहले inform → बाद में documentation**, जहाँ applicable हो।

---

# 4. Communication Policy

Company communication के लिए:

### Internal

- Task system
- Team chat
- Email
- Meetings

### Rule

**Important decisions chat में खोए नहीं रहने चाहिए।**

Decision को:

**Decision Log / Project System**

में record किया जाएगा।

---

# 5. Meeting Policy

Meeting तभी जब:

**Async communication sufficient न हो।**

हर meeting में:

- Agenda
- Participants
- Decision
- Action items
- Owner
- Deadline

होना चाहिए।

---

# 6. Approval Matrix

यह बहुत important है।

| Decision | Approval |
|---|---|
| Small routine expense | Manager |
| Department expense | Department Head |
| Major expense | COO |
| Strategic expense | CEO |
| New senior hire | COO/CEO |
| Vendor contract | Authorized management |
| Client scope change | Delivery + commercial owner |
| Security-sensitive access | Authorized technical/security owner |

Exact ₹ limits company budget के अनुसार अलग रखे जाएँ।

---

# 7. Client Communication Policy

Employee client से:

❌ Unauthorized pricing promise

❌ Contractual commitment

❌ Scope expansion promise

❌ Delivery date guarantee

नहीं करेगा।

जब तक authority assigned न हो।

Client communication:

**Account/PM → Client**

---

# 8. Confidentiality

Employee/vendor को सिर्फ वही information मिले जिसकी role के लिए जरूरत है।

Principle:

> **Need-to-know access.**

Confidential information:

- Client data
- Pricing
- Internal SOPs
- Credentials
- Business strategy
- Employee data
- Financial information

को unauthorized sharing से protect करना होगा।

---

# 9. Social Media Policy

Employee personal account पर company representation करते समय:

- Confidential information share नहीं
- Client information बिना permission नहीं
- Internal screenshots नहीं
- False company claims नहीं

Official company account:

**Marketing/authorized owner**

handle करेगा।

---

# 10. Performance Policy

Performance evaluation:

**KPI + Quality + Reliability + Ownership**

पर होगा।

Manager को:

**personal favoritism**

के आधार पर rating नहीं देनी चाहिए।

---

# 11. Grievance / Complaint

Employee के पास escalation path होना चाहिए:

```text id="griev1"
Employee
 ↓
Team Lead
 ↓
Department Head / HR
 ↓
COO
 ↓
CEO (serious cases)
```

और sensitive complaints के लिए appropriate confidential channel होना चाहिए।

---

# 12. Conflict of Interest

Employee को disclose करना चाहिए अगर:

- Personal business conflict हो
- Vendor से personal financial relationship हो
- Client से unauthorized relationship हो
- Hiring candidate से significant personal relationship हो

Company को situation के अनुसार mitigation/recusal तय करना चाहिए।

---

# 13. Vendor Policy

Vendor:

- Approved scope में काम करेगा
- Unauthorized subcontracting नहीं करेगा, जहाँ contract इसे restrict करता हो
- Confidential data protect करेगा
- Approved communication channel use करेगा
- Deliverable standards follow करेगा

---

# 14. Quality Policy

हर department के लिए:

**Output → QA → Approval**

जहाँ applicable हो।

Employee:

> “काम पूरा”

कहने से task automatically approved नहीं माना जाएगा।

---

# 15. Security Policy

Basic rules:

- Shared passwords नहीं
- Personal devices पर sensitive data के लिए defined controls
- MFA जहाँ उपलब्ध हो
- Access role-based
- Former employees का access promptly revoke
- Credentials को approved password manager में रखना
- Security incident immediately report करना

---

# 16. Data Policy

Data lifecycle:

```text id="data1"
Collect
 ↓
Use
 ↓
Store
 ↓
Access Control
 ↓
Retention
 ↓
Delete/Archive
```

Client data को जरूरत से ज्यादा समय तक नहीं रखना चाहिए।

Applicable privacy/data-protection requirements को jurisdiction और client contract के हिसाब से incorporate करना होगा।

---

# 17. Disciplinary Framework

हर mistake punishment नहीं।

### Level 1

Coaching

### Level 2

Formal warning

### Level 3

Performance improvement / corrective action

### Level 4

Further disciplinary action, up to separation, according to policy, contract and applicable law.

**Serious misconduct/security breach** अलग escalation trigger हो सकता है।

---

# 18. Offboarding Policy

जब employee/vendor छोड़ता है:

```text id="off1"
Exit notification
 ↓
Handover
 ↓
Project transfer
 ↓
Asset recovery
 ↓
Access revocation
 ↓
Credential rotation if required
 ↓
Final HR/Finance process
 ↓
Exit record
```

---

# 19. Policy Ownership

| Policy Area | Owner |
|---|---|
| HR | HR |
| Operations | COO |
| Technology | CTO |
| Security | CTO/authorized security owner |
| Finance | Finance |
| Client delivery | Delivery Head |
| Sales | Sales Head |
| Marketing | Growth/Marketing Head |

CEO को हर policy खुद maintain नहीं करनी है।

---

# 20. Policy Review

हर policy के लिए:

**Owner + Version + Last Updated + Next Review**

रहे।

उदाहरण:

> HR-003  
> Leave Policy  
> v1.2  
> Owner: HR  
> Last Review: Aug 2026  
> Next Review: Feb 2027

---

# 21. Employee Handbook

इन सभी policies को अंत में एक:

**Nivy Next Employee Handbook**

में consolidate किया जा सकता है।

Employee joining पर:

**Read → Acknowledge → Ask Questions → Accept**

process होगा।

message time: 2026-08-22 09:26:58

# chatgpt response

ठीक है। अब **Module 21 — Security, Access Control & Offboarding System** बनाते हैं। तुम्हारे model में यह बहुत जरूरी है, क्योंकि employees + vendors + outsourced execution बढ़ने पर access control कमजोर हुआ तो पूरा operation risk में आ सकता है।

# Nivy Next — Security & Access System

## 1. Golden Rule

> **जिस व्यक्ति को जितना access चाहिए, उतना ही access दो — उससे ज्यादा नहीं।**

इसे **Least Privilege** कहते हैं।

---

# 2. Access Levels

| Level | Access |
|---|---|
| **L1** | सिर्फ अपने काम के tools/data |
| **L2** | अपने workflow + limited team data |
| **L3** | Team/department data |
| **L4** | Department-level systems |
| **L5/Executive** | Business-critical access |

हर employee को default में maximum access नहीं मिलेगा।

---

# 3. Example

मान लो Lead Researcher को CRM में leads भरनी हैं।

उसे:

✅ Lead database

✅ Assigned CRM area

✅ Research tools

चाहिए।

लेकिन:

❌ Finance

❌ Payroll

❌ Client contracts

❌ Password vault का पूरा access

❌ दूसरे departments का confidential data

नहीं चाहिए।

---

# 4. Access Request Process

कोई employee खुद access नहीं लेगा।

```text id="acc1"
Employee Request
      ↓
Manager Approval
      ↓
System Owner Approval
      ↓
IT/Tech Provisioning
      ↓
Access Logged
```

Critical access में additional approval हो सकता है।

---

# 5. Joiner Process

नया employee:

**HR confirms joining**

↓

Manager role/access matrix भेजता है

↓

IT/Tech accounts बनाता है

↓

MFA enable

↓

Required tools access

↓

Access record update

↓

Employee starts work

---

# 6. Access Matrix

एक master spreadsheet/database:

| Employee | Role | CRM | Email | Finance | Automation | Client Data |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L1 Researcher | Research | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ | Limited |
| L2 Automation | Automation | Limited | ✅ | ❌ | ✅ | Project-only |
| L3 Team Lead | Team | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | Limited | Team |
| COO | Operations | ✅ | ✅ | Limited | Oversight | Broad |

**Actual permissions tool-by-tool define होंगे।**

---

# 7. Vendor Access

Vendor को:

**temporary + project-specific access**

देना बेहतर है।

Example:

Client Project A

→ Vendor A

→ Project A folder

→ Project A credentials

→ Expiry date

Project खत्म:

**Access revoke.**

---

# 8. Credential Management

Passwords को random WhatsApp/Slack/email messages में share नहीं करना चाहिए।

Use:

**Approved password manager + MFA + role-based access**

और critical credentials को individual ownership से हटाकर company-controlled system में रखना चाहिए।

---

# 9. Access Review

### Monthly

Department Heads देखें:

> किसके पास क्या access है?

### Quarterly

Formal access review:

- Still required?
- Too much access?
- Role changed?
- Vendor still active?
- Former employees removed?

---

# 10. Offboarding — Employee

जब employee छोड़ता है:

### Immediately / as appropriate

- Email access revoke
- CRM access revoke
- Cloud access revoke
- Automation access revoke
- Project tools revoke
- Passwords/credentials rotate where necessary
- Sessions/tokens revoke
- Devices recover
- Documents transfer

---

# 11. Offboarding Checklist

| Item | Done |
|---|---|
| HR exit confirmed | ☐ |
| Manager notified | ☐ |
| Handover complete | ☐ |
| Projects transferred | ☐ |
| Email disabled | ☐ |
| CRM disabled | ☐ |
| Cloud access removed | ☐ |
| Automation access removed | ☐ |
| MFA/session tokens reviewed | ☐ |
| Assets recovered | ☐ |
| Credentials rotated if required | ☐ |
| Final finance/HR process | ☐ |

---

# 12. Vendor Offboarding

```text id="venoff"
Contract ends
 ↓
Outstanding work identified
 ↓
Files transferred
 ↓
Client data returned/deleted as required
 ↓
Access revoked
 ↓
Credentials rotated if required
 ↓
Invoice reconciled
 ↓
Vendor status changed
```

---

# 13. Security Incident

अगर कोई बोले:

> “मुझे लगता है client data गलत जगह चला गया।”

तो employee खुद investigation करने की कोशिश नहीं करेगा।

Flow:

```text id="secflow"
Incident Detected
 ↓
Immediately Report
 ↓
Security/CTO Owner
 ↓
Containment
 ↓
Evidence Preservation
 ↓
Impact Assessment
 ↓
Management Decision
 ↓
Client/Regulatory Communication if required
 ↓
Root Cause
 ↓
Corrective Action
```

Serious incidents में applicable legal/contractual notification requirements भी follow करनी होंगी।

---

# 14. Audit Logs

Critical systems में जहाँ संभव हो:

- Login
- Permission changes
- Data export
- Record deletion
- Workflow changes
- Credential changes

का audit trail रखना चाहिए।

इससे बाद में पता चल सके:

**किसने क्या किया और कब किया।**

---

# 15. Security Ownership

| Area | Owner |
|---|---|
| Employee access | HR + IT/Tech |
| Client data | Delivery + Tech |
| Infrastructure | CTO |
| Automation credentials | CTO/Automation Lead |
| Financial systems | Finance |
| Vendor access | Vendor Manager + System Owner |
| Security incidents | CTO/authorized security owner |
| Overall business risk | COO/CEO |

---

# 16. Security KPI

| KPI | Target |
|---|---:|
| Critical systems with MFA | 100% where supported |
| Unauthorized active accounts | 0 |
| Former employee access | 0 |
| Access reviews completed | 100% |
| Critical security incidents | 0 |
| Offboarding completion | 100% |
| High-risk access exceptions | 0/unresolved |

---

# 17. सबसे जरूरी बात

तुम्हारी company में:

**Employee → काम**

**Team Lead → team**

**Department Head → department**

**COO → operations**

**CTO → technology/security**

**CEO → business**

लेकिन:

> **कोई एक व्यक्ति पूरी company के सारे passwords और systems का master access लेकर नहीं बैठेगा।**

यह बहुत बड़ा single point of failure बन सकता है।

# chatgpt response

हाँ। अब **Module 22 — Complete Automation + AI Operating Map** बनाते हैं। यही layer तय करेगी कि Nivy Next में **कहाँ इंसान काम करेगा, कहाँ AI, कहाँ n8n/automation, और कहाँ final human approval जरूरी रहेगा।**

# Nivy Next — Automation Architecture

## 1. सबसे पहले 4-layer rule

हर काम को इन चार categories में डालेंगे:

| Layer | काम कौन करेगा |
|---|---|
| **A — Human Decision** | Strategy, judgment, negotiation, approval |
| **B — Human Execution** | जहाँ quality/judgment जरूरी है |
| **C — AI Assisted** | Research, drafting, analysis, classification |
| **D — Fully Automated** | Repetitive data movement, alerts, reports |

**Rule:** High-risk decisions को बिना human approval के fully automate नहीं करना।

---

- Vision

```text
       ↓
Exception alert
```

CEO को हर छोटी activity नहीं दिखेगी।

उसे सिर्फ:

**Revenue / Profit / Growth / Risk / Major Problems**

दिखेंगे।

# 22. अब सबसे महत्वपूर्ण बात

नहीं?

नहीं?

`क्या measure होगा → data कहाँ से आएगा → कौन verify करेगा → target क्या होगा → red/yellow/green threshold क्या होगा`

ताकि Team Lead data manipulate न कर सके।

**🔴 4. Master Hiring Pack**  
हर role के लिए ready-to-use:

`JD + Required Skills + Screening Questions + Skill Test + Interview Scorecard + Paid Trial + Evaluation + Offer Level`

---

### और एक important distinction

हमारे पास अभी **“framework” बहुत हद तक तैयार है**, लेकिन **“ready-to-use company documents” अभी पूरी तरह तैयार नहीं हैं।**

यानी मैं अब आगे सिर्फ यह नहीं कहूँगा कि *“Module complete.”*

अब हर module को actual operating document में convert करेंगे।

### Overall Progress

**Conceptual Operating System:** 🟢 लगभग complete  
**Actual Operating Library:** 🟡 अभी consolidation बाकी  
**Ready-to-hire documents:** 🟡 काफी हिस्सा बाकी  
**Ready-to-run SOPs:** 🟡 बाकी  
**Master dashboards/scorecards:** 🟡 बाकी

और हाँ—**progress tracker आगे हर document के साथ update होगा**, ताकि तुम्हें हर बार पूछना न पड़े कि क्या बना और क्या बाकी है।

# B. COO — Detailed JD

### Purpose

पूरी company को ऐसा operating system देना जिसमें CEO को हर छोटी चीज़ manually manage न करनी पड़े।

### Responsibilities

- Department coordination
- KPI review
- Capacity planning
- Process improvement
- Escalation management
- Vendor coordination
- Delivery oversight
- Management reporting
- SOP enforcement

### Skills

- Operations management
- Data analysis
- Process design
- People management
- Project management
- Business understanding

### Qualification

- Graduate preferred
- Operations/business/management background useful
- Strong practical experience ज्यादा महत्वपूर्ण

### Experience

**5+ years preferred** for a mature organization; startup stage में strong L3/L4 operator भी यह responsibility संभाल सकता है।

### KPI

| KPI | Target |
|---|---:|
| On-time delivery | ≥95% |
| Critical unresolved escalations | 0 |
| KPI reporting completion | 100% |
| Major process failures | Minimize |
| Department SLA compliance | ≥95% |

### Hiring Test

Candidate को:

> “50-person agency में तीन departments लगातार deadline miss कर रहे हैं। Diagnose करके 30-day recovery plan बनाओ।”

Evaluate:

- Diagnosis
- Prioritization
- Metrics
- Delegation
- Escalation
- Implementation plan

---

- Technology roadmap

- Software architecture
- APIs
- Automation

- Security
- Technical leadership

### KPI

- System uptime
- Automation success rate
- Workflow failure rate
- Delivery SLA
- Security incidents
- Automation ROI

> “Lead → CRM → AI qualification → outreach → reporting workflow का architecture design करो।”

Evaluate:

- Architecture

- Security
- Scalability
- Monitoring

# 1. Lead Research SOP

**SOP ID:** GROW-001  
**Owner:** Lead Researcher L1  
**Reviewer:** Research Lead L3

### Workflow

```text
Target ICP
 ↓
Company Search
 ↓
Company Verification
 ↓
Contact Identification
 ↓
Data Enrichment
 ↓
Duplicate Check
 ↓
CRM Entry
 ↓
QA Sample
```

### L1 करेगा

- Company research
- Contact research
- Required fields भरना
- Source record रखना
- CRM update

### L2 करेगा

- Complex accounts
- Data verification
- Exceptions
- L1 output review

### L3 करेगा

- ICP definition
- Process design
- Quality monitoring
- Team productivity

### QA

Random sample independently verify होगा।

### KPI

- Accuracy
- Validity
- Completeness
- SLA
- Duplicate rate

---

# 2. Outreach SOP

**SOP ID:** SALES-001

```text
Approved Lead
 ↓
Qualification
 ↓
Personalization
 ↓
Message Review
 ↓
Send
 ↓
Response Detection
 ↓
CRM Update
 ↓
Follow-up
```

### L1

Data preparation + CRM.

### L2

Outreach execution + qualification.

### L3

Messaging strategy + performance.

### Human approval

High-value/custom outreach में mandatory।

# 3. Lead Qualification SOP

Lead आने के बाद:

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| ICP fit | Yes/No |
| Need | Yes/No |
| Authority | Yes/No |
| Budget | Known/Unknown |
| Timeline | Known/Unknown |

फिर:

**Unqualified → Nurture**

**Qualified → Sales**

# 4. Sales Call SOP

### Before Call

- Account research
- Previous communication
- Meeting objective
- Questions

### During Call

- Situation
- Problem
- Impact
- Desired outcome
- Decision process
- Timeline

### After Call

- Summary
- CRM update
- Next action
- Owner
- Deadline

AI call summary बना सकता है, लेकिन salesperson CRM accuracy के लिए responsible रहेगा।

```text
 ↓

 ↓

 ↓

 ↓

 ↓

 ↓

 ↓
```

**Pricing exceptions → authorized approval.**

Salesperson अकेले non-standard pricing approve नहीं करेगा।

# 7. Project Task Assignment SOP

PM:

1. Deliverable identify करेगा
2. Work breakdown करेगा
3. L1/L2/L3 assign करेगा
4. Deadline देगा
5. Dependencies define करेगा
6. QA checkpoint बनाएगा

### Rule

**Task बिना owner + deadline के “created” नहीं माना जाएगा।**

# 8. Execution SOP

हर execution task:

```text
Brief
 ↓
Understand requirement
 ↓
Execute
 ↓
Self-check
 ↓
Evidence attach
 ↓
Submit
```

Employee सिर्फ:

> “Done”

नहीं लिखेगा।

उसे **evidence/output** attach करना होगा।

# 9. QA SOP

**SOP ID:** QA-001

```text

 ↓

 ↓
Checklist
 ↓

 ↓

 ├── YES → Approved
 └── NO → Rework
```

**Accuracy**

**Completeness**

**Requirement compliance**

---

# 10. Rework SOP

QA failure:

**Reason Code**

दिया जाएगा।

उदाहरण:

- R01 — Accuracy
- R02 — Missing requirement
- R03 — Formatting
- R04 — Functional defect
- R05 — Scope mismatch

फिर task वापस employee को।

इससे बाद में पता चलेगा:

> कौन-सी team बार-बार किस प्रकार की गलती कर रही है।

## Nivy Next — Hiring Assessment System

हर candidate को **100 points** पर evaluate किया जाएगा।

| Assessment | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Basic Screening | 10 |
| Skill Test | 30 |
| Structured Interview | 25 |
| Practical/Paid Trial | 25 |
| Culture/Ownership | 10 |
| **Total** | **100** |

### Hiring decision

- **85–100:** Strong Hire
- **75–84:** Hire
- **65–74:** Hold / second assessment
- **<65:** No Hire

**लेकिन critical skill में fail होने पर total score अच्छा होने के बावजूद candidate reject हो सकता है।**

# 1. Lead Researcher — L1

उसे निकालना होगा:

- Designation
- Contact information
- Relevant source

| Area | Points |
|---|---:|
| Accuracy | 12 |
| Completeness | 6 |
| Research quality | 6 |
| Speed | 3 |
| Formatting | 3 |

### Interview

पूछो:

1. अगर information दो websites पर अलग मिले तो क्या करोगे?
2. Duplicate lead कैसे पहचानोगे?
3. किसी contact का email verify कैसे करोगे?
4. अगर target पूरा करने के लिए quality गिरानी पड़े तो?

**Red flag:** “मैं quantity पूरी कर दूँगा, quality बाद में देखेंगे।”

# Universal Interview Scorecard

हर interviewer यही form भरेगा:

| Competency | Score /5 |
|---|---:|
| Technical/Functional Skill | /5 |
| Problem Solving | /5 |
| Communication | /5 |
| Ownership | /5 |
| Attention to Detail | /5 |
| Learning Ability | /5 |

Interviewer's personal feeling:

> “मुझे candidate अच्छा लगा।”

**score नहीं है।**

हर decision:

**Evidence + Score + Practical Test**

पर आधारित होगा।

# chatgpt response

बिल्कुल। अब **Salary + Compensation Matrix** बनाते हैं। इसे fixed “हर व्यक्ति को यही salary” की तरह नहीं, बल्कि **level-based salary bands** की तरह रखना बेहतर है, ताकि role, skill और experience के हिसाब से offer किया जा सके।

# Nivy Next — L1–L5 Compensation Framework

### Level Philosophy

| Level | Meaning | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| **L1** | Executor | SOP के अनुसार काम |
| **L2** | Independent Specialist | अपने output का ownership |
| **L3** | Lead/Manager | Team/process/result ownership |
| **L4** | Department Head | Department business result |
| **L5** | Executive | Company-level result |

---

## 1. Suggested Monthly Salary Bands — India

ये **planning bands** हैं, final offer market, city, role scarcity और candidate quality के हिसाब से adjust होगा।

| Level | Monthly Gross Range | Typical Role |
|---|---:|---|
| **L1** | ₹18k–₹30k | Assistant, Researcher, Coordinator |
| **L2** | ₹30k–₹55k | Specialist, Executive, Developer |
| **L3** | ₹55k–₹1.0L | Team Lead, PM, Senior Specialist |
| **L4** | ₹1.0L–₹2.0L | Department Head |
| **L5** | ₹2.0L–₹4.0L+ | CXO / Executive |

ये broad operating ranges हैं; technical/sales roles में market और variable compensation के कारण काफी variation हो सकता है।

---

# 2. L1 Structure

### Example — Lead Researcher

**Band:** ₹18k–₹30k

### Starting offer

Candidate की skill test performance के अनुसार:

- Basic → lower band
- Good → mid band
- Excellent → upper band

लेकिन केवल interview अच्छा होने से upper band नहीं देना।

---

# 3. L2 Structure

Example:

**Automation Developer**

₹30k–₹55k

लेकिन specialized technical roles के लिए market premium अलग हो सकता है।

L2 का principle:

> **Independent output के लिए pay.**

---

# 4. L3 Structure

Example:

**Sales Team Lead / Project Manager / QA Lead**

₹55k–₹1L

L3 को सिर्फ individual productivity पर नहीं:

**Team KPI + process KPI + business result**

पर evaluate किया जाएगा।

---

# 5. L4 Structure

Department Head:

**₹1L–₹2L+**

यहाँ compensation में variable component meaningful हो सकता है।

Example:

**Base + Performance Bonus**

---

# 6. L5 Structure

CEO/COO/CTO/CFO जैसे executive positions के लिए simple salary band पर्याप्त नहीं होता।

Structure:

**Base + Performance Incentive + Long-term incentive/equity where appropriate**

और founder/CEO compensation को employee salary matrix से अलग रखना बेहतर है।

---

# 7. Sales Compensation

Sales में variable pay important है।

Example structure:

**Base + Commission/Bonus**

लेकिन commission को:

**collected/recognized revenue + contract rules**

से tie करना चाहिए, सिर्फ verbal booking से नहीं।

---

# 8. Performance Rating

हर review cycle:

| Rating | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **5** | Exceptional |
| **4** | Exceeds expectations |
| **3** | Meets expectations |
| **2** | Below expectations |
| **1** | Unsatisfactory |

फिर:

**Performance + Level + Market + Role**

के आधार पर compensation review।

---

# 9. Promotion Rule

सिर्फ इसलिए promotion नहीं:

> “वह बहुत समय से L1 है।”

Promotion तब:

**Current-level performance + next-level capability + role availability**

तीनों satisfy हों।

उदाहरण:

### L1 → L2

Candidate:

- SOP independently follow करता है
- Problems solve करता है
- Quality maintain करता है
- Consistent KPI देता है
- दूसरों से लगातार hand-holding नहीं मांगता

### L2 → L3

Candidate:

- दूसरों को guide करता है
- Process improve करता है
- Escalations संभालता है
- Team/output own करता है
- Metrics समझता है

---

# 10. Salary Increase

Salary increase को automatic annual increment मत बनाओ।

Possible framework:

**Performance review + market correction + promotion + retention considerations**

अलग-अलग चीजें हैं।

---

# 11. Compensation Approval Matrix

| Change | Approval |
|---|---|
| L1 normal hiring | Hiring Manager + HR |
| L2/L3 hiring | Department Head + HR |
| L4 hiring | COO/CEO + HR |
| L5/executive | CEO/Board-level authority as applicable |
| Promotion | Manager + Department Head + HR |
| Major salary exception | COO/CEO |

---

# 12. Compensation Data Confidentiality

हर employee को दूसरे employee की salary देखने का unrestricted access नहीं होना चाहिए।

लेकिन company को:

- Fair pay
- Consistent bands
- Documented exceptions
- Legal compliance

maintain करनी चाहिए।

---

# 13. Complete Example

मान लो तुम्हें **10 Lead Researchers** hire करने हैं।

Band:

**₹18k–₹30k**

Candidate A:

Skill Test 72/100 → ₹20k

Candidate B:

Skill Test 86/100 → ₹25k

Candidate C:

Skill Test 95/100 + relevant experience → ₹28k

फिर performance के आधार पर आगे बढ़ेगा।

इससे:

**“सबको same salary”**

और

**“जिसने negotiate किया उसे ज्यादा”**

दोनों problems कम होती हैं।

---

# सबसे महत्वपूर्ण Hiring Rule

**Revenue से पहले headcount नहीं बढ़ाना।**

जब existing team का:

- Capacity utilization high हो
- SLA consistently meet हो
- Revenue support करे
- Process documented हो

तभी next person hire करें।

---

# Workforce Planning Formula

हर department के लिए:

**Required Headcount = Forecast Workload ÷ Sustainable Capacity per Person**

फिर:

**+ small capacity buffer**

रखेंगे।

इससे “लगता है एक और आदमी चाहिए” वाली hiring खत्म होगी।

# chatgpt response

ठीक है। अब **Module 24 — Payroll + Unit Economics + Break-even Model** बनाते हैं। अभी इसे planning model रख रहा हूँ; actual statutory employer costs, taxes और benefits को final payroll setup से पहले CA/payroll specialist से validate करना होगा।

# Nivy Next — Payroll & Unit Economics

## 1. Lean Team — 18 People

Planning के लिए mid-point salary assumptions लेते हैं:

| Function | People | Avg. Monthly Salary | Monthly Cost |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Operations/Management | 2 | ₹1,50,000 | ₹3,00,000 |
| Sales | 3 | ₹50,000 | ₹1,50,000 |
| Lead Generation | 3 | ₹30,000 | ₹90,000 |
| Marketing | 2 | ₹50,000 | ₹1,00,000 |
| Delivery | 3 | ₹50,000 | ₹1,50,000 |
| Technology | 2 | ₹75,000 | ₹1,50,000 |
| QA | 1 | ₹50,000 | ₹50,000 |
| HR/Admin | 1 | ₹40,000 | ₹40,000 |
| Finance | 1 | ₹45,000 | ₹45,000 |
| Vendor | Shared | — | — |
| **Total** | **18** | | **₹10.75L/month** |

So base salary planning roughly:

**₹10.75 lakh/month**

और annualized:

**₹1.29 crore/year**

---

# 2. Real Employer Cost

Salary ही पूरा cost नहीं है।

Budget में अलग से रखना होगा:

- Employer statutory contributions जहाँ applicable हों
- Benefits
- Recruiting cost
- Software
- Equipment
- Training
- Office/remote infrastructure
- Contractor/vendor cost

इसलिए planning में एक **loaded payroll factor** रखना बेहतर है।

अगर illustrative **15% buffer** रखें:

**₹10.75L × 1.15 ≈ ₹12.36L/month**

यह सिर्फ planning assumption है, statutory calculation नहीं।

---

# 3. Department Cost

Approximate monthly salary allocation:

| Department | Cost |
|---|---:|
| Management | ₹3.00L |
| Sales | ₹1.50L |
| Lead Gen | ₹0.90L |
| Marketing | ₹1.00L |
| Delivery | ₹1.50L |
| Technology | ₹1.50L |
| QA | ₹0.50L |
| HR/Admin | ₹0.40L |
| Finance | ₹0.45L |
| **Total** | **₹10.75L** |

इसमें outsourced vendor spend शामिल नहीं है।

---

# 4. Revenue Requirement

अब सबसे important calculation।

मान लो:

**Fixed operating cost = ₹15L/month**

और contribution margin after direct delivery/vendor costs:

**60%**

तो break-even revenue:

**₹15L ÷ 0.60 = ₹25L/month**

यानी लगभग:

### **₹25 lakh monthly revenue**

पर operating break-even।

---

# 5. Example Client Economics

मान लो average client:

**₹2.5L/month**

और contribution margin:

**60%**

तो contribution:

**₹1.5L/client/month**

अगर fixed cost:

**₹15L**

तो:

**10 clients × ₹1.5L = ₹15L**

लगभग break-even।

इसलिए सिर्फ client count नहीं देखना।

**Contribution per client** देखना ज्यादा useful है।

---

# 6. Three Business Scenarios

| Scenario | Monthly Revenue | Contribution Margin | Contribution | Fixed Cost | Result |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Conservative | ₹20L | 55% | ₹11L | ₹15L | -₹4L |
| Base | ₹30L | 60% | ₹18L | ₹15L | +₹3L |
| Strong | ₹40L | 65% | ₹26L | ₹15L | +₹11L |

ये **illustrative scenarios** हैं, actual Nivy Next economics नहीं।

---

# 7. Hiring Trigger

नई hire सिर्फ इसलिए नहीं:

> “Team busy है।”

Trigger होना चाहिए:

### Condition 1
Existing capacity ≥ ~80–85%

### Condition 2
Demand predictable है

### Condition 3
SOP documented है

### Condition 4
New hire का expected contribution cost justify करता है

### Condition 5
Cash runway पर्याप्त है

---

# 8. Team Lead Economics

Team Lead को भी cost center की तरह देखना है।

अगर:

**Team Lead cost = ₹80k**

तो उसके नीचे की team से सिर्फ activity नहीं बल्कि measurable output चाहिए।

उदाहरण:

> Lead generation team का verified output → qualified pipeline → sales opportunities → revenue.

यही **output chain** है।

---

# 9. CEO Dashboard

तुम्हारे dashboard पर ideally ये numbers दिखने चाहिए:

```text id="dash9"
Revenue
   ↓
Gross Revenue
   ↓
Direct Delivery Cost
   ↓
Contribution Margin
   ↓
Payroll
   ↓
Operating Expenses
   ↓
EBITDA / Operating Profit
```

और साथ में:

**Revenue / Employee**

**Gross Profit / Employee**

**Client Revenue / Delivery FTE**

**CAC**

**LTV**

**Utilization**

---

# 10. सबसे Important Metric

तुम्हारी outsourced + automation-heavy company में मैं एक metric जरूर रखूँगा:

### Revenue per Internal FTE

उदाहरण:

₹30L monthly revenue ÷ 18 people

≈ **₹1.67L revenue/FTE/month**

फिर जैसे automation और outsourcing improve हों:

₹40L ÷ 18

≈ **₹2.22L/FTE/month**

इससे पता चलेगा कि organization वास्तव में scalable हो रही है या सिर्फ headcount बढ़ रहा है।

# Nivy Next — Master Operating Library v1.0

## 00. Company Operating Architecture

```text
CEO
│
├── COO / Operations
│   ├── Sales
│   ├── Marketing / Growth
│   ├── Delivery
│   ├── HR
│   ├── Finance
│   ├── Vendor Management
│   └── QA
│
└── CTO
    ├── Automation
    ├── Technology
    └── Security
```

**QA को independent control function** रखा जाएगा।

---

# 01. Organization Structure

हर department में hierarchy:

```text
L4 Department Head
        ↓
L3 Team Lead / Manager
        ↓
L2 Specialist
        ↓
L1 Executor
```

CEO/COO/CTO जैसे executive positions L5 हैं।

---

# 02. Role Architecture

हर employee का role document में ये fields mandatory:

**Role → Level → Reports To → Purpose → Responsibilities → Skills → Qualification → KPI → Salary Band → Hiring Test → Promotion Criteria**

इससे कोई employee यह नहीं कह सकता:

> “मुझे पता नहीं था मेरा काम क्या है।”

---

# 03. Department Architecture

### Sales
Lead → Qualification → Meeting → Proposal → Negotiation → Close → Collection

### Marketing/Growth
Research → Content/Campaign → Traffic → Lead → Funnel → Optimization

### Lead Generation
ICP → Research → Verification → Enrichment → QA → CRM

### Delivery
Client Onboarding → Planning → Execution → QA → Delivery → Feedback → Renewal

### Technology
Requirement → Design → Build → Test → QA → Production → Monitoring

### QA
Sampling → Testing → Scoring → Defect → Rework → Verification → Reporting

### HR
Workforce Need → JD → Hiring → Onboarding → Performance → Development → Exit

### Finance
Billing → Collection → Payables → Reconciliation → Reporting → Controls

### Vendor
Requirement → Sourcing → Test → Pilot → QA → Acceptance → Payment → Rating

---

# 04. Hiring Operating System

हर vacancy:

```text
Hiring Request
↓
Budget Approval
↓
JD
↓
Sourcing
↓
Screening
↓
Skill Test
↓
Interview
↓
Paid Trial
↓
Scorecard
↓
Approval
↓
Offer
↓
Onboarding
```

### Hiring Score

| Component | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Screening | 10% |
| Skill Test | 30% |
| Interview | 25% |
| Practical Trial | 25% |
| Ownership/Culture | 10% |
| **Total** | **100%** |

---

# 05. L1–L5 System

| Level | Expected Behaviour |
|---|---|
| L1 | SOP execute करे |
| L2 | Independent output own करे |
| L3 | Team/process own करे |
| L4 | Department result own करे |
| L5 | Company result own करे |

### Golden principle

**Responsibility बढ़ने के साथ decision-making authority भी बढ़नी चाहिए।**

---

# 06. Compensation

Planning bands:

| Level | Planning Range |
|---|---:|
| L1 | ₹18k–₹30k |
| L2 | ₹30k–₹55k |
| L3 | ₹55k–₹1L |
| L4 | ₹1L–₹2L |
| L5 | ₹2L–₹4L+ |

ये **planning bands** हैं; actual offer role, market, geography, experience और scarcity के अनुसार तय होगा।

Sales roles में:

**Base + Variable**

जहाँ appropriate हो।

---

# 07. KPI System

हर KPI के साथ:

**Definition → Formula → Data Source → Owner → Verifier → Target → Threshold**

Example:

### Lead Accuracy

`Valid Leads ÷ Audited Leads × 100`

Team Lead report करेगा।

**QA independently verify करेगा।**

---

# 08. Reporting System

Employee:

**Output + Evidence**

↓

Team Lead:

**Review**

↓

System:

**Data**

↓

QA:

**Independent Sample**

↓

Department Head:

**Performance Review**

↓

COO:

**Management Dashboard**

---

# 09. Anti-Manipulation Control

यह तुम्हारी original concern के लिए critical है।

Team Lead अगर बोले:

> “हमने 1,000 leads complete किए।”

तो dashboard सिर्फ उस claim को accept नहीं करेगा।

System देखेगा:

**Submitted → Validated → QA Verified → Accepted**

और KPI:

### **Verified Output**

पर आधारित होगा।

---

# 10. SOP Governance

हर SOP में:

- SOP ID
- Owner
- Version
- Trigger
- Input
- Steps
- Output
- QA
- Approval
- SLA
- Escalation
- Evidence
- Change Log

---

# 11. QA System

```text
Employee Output
↓
Self Check
↓
Team Lead
↓
Independent QA
↓
Pass / Rework
↓
Final Acceptance
```

High-risk work में deeper QA sampling।

---

# 12. Client Delivery

```text
Sale
↓
Handoff
↓
Onboarding
↓
Requirements
↓
Project Plan
↓
Execution
↓
QA
↓
Client Delivery
↓
Feedback
↓
Revision
↓
Acceptance
↓
Renewal/Upsell
```

---

# 13. Outsourcing System

Outsource तभी:

**जब task clearly defined + measurable + QA-able हो।**

Workflow:

```text
Requirement
↓
Vendor
↓
Test
↓
Paid Pilot
↓
QA
↓
Acceptance
↓
Payment
↓
Vendor Score
```

---

# 14. Technology & Automation

Production changes:

**Build → Test → QA → Approval → Deploy → Monitor**

No uncontrolled production changes.

---

# 15. HR System

HR owns:

- Hiring process
- Employee records
- Onboarding
- Training coordination
- Performance cycle
- Policies
- Offboarding

Department Heads own:

**actual employee performance.**

HR performance manager नहीं है; HR people-system owner है।

---

# 16. Finance Controls

Finance tracks:

**Revenue → Receivables → Payables → Payroll → Cash Flow → Reporting**

Critical financial decisions require appropriate approval authority.

---

# 17. Workforce Planning

Headcount formula:

**Required Capacity ÷ Sustainable Capacity per Employee + Buffer**

Hire only when:

- workload is sustained,
- current capacity is insufficient,
- SOP exists,
- economics support the hire.

---

# 18. Lean Starting Organization

Initial planning:

**~18 people**

Full structure:

**~42 positions**

लेकिन 42 positions का मतलब 42 people immediately hire करना नहीं है।

पहले revenue और workload के साथ organization scale होगी।

---

# 19. Management Dashboard

CEO/COO dashboard पर:

### Financial

- Revenue
- Gross Margin
- Contribution Margin
- Payroll
- Operating Cost
- Cash Flow
- Profit

### Sales

- Pipeline
- Meetings
- Conversion
- Win Rate
- Revenue
- Collections

### Delivery

- On-time %
- QA %
- Rework
- Client Satisfaction
- Retention

### People

- Headcount
- Utilization
- Attrition
- Hiring Pipeline
- Performance Distribution

### Growth

- Leads
- Qualified Leads
- CAC
- Conversion
- Campaign ROI

### Operations

- SLA
- Productivity
- Escalations
- Automation Success Rate

---

# 20. Decision Rights

यह बहुत जरूरी है।

### L1

**Do**

### L2

**Do + Solve**

### L3

**Do + Solve + Allocate**

### L4

**Do + Solve + Allocate + Decide**

### L5

**Set Direction + Decide**

इससे हर छोटी बात CEO तक नहीं आएगी।

---

# 21. Escalation

```text
L1
 ↓
L2
 ↓
L3
 ↓
L4
 ↓
COO
 ↓
CEO
```

लेकिन **critical financial, legal, security या reputational issues** predefined rules के अनुसार सीधे higher authority तक escalate हो सकते हैं।

---

# 22. Promotion

Promotion केवल tenure से नहीं।

### L1 → L2

**Consistent execution + independent problem solving**

### L2 → L3

**Process/team ownership + leadership**

### L3 → L4

**Department-level business results**

### L4 → L5

**Company-level strategic impact**

---

# 23. Master Document Control

हर operating document का:

**Owner + Version + Date + Approval + Review Date**

होगा।

इससे पुराना SOP silently circulate नहीं करेगा।

Employee खुद check करे।

**Layer 2 — Team Lead**

Operational review।

**Layer 3 — Independent QA**

Random/targeted audit।

---

# QA Sampling Example

मान लो:

**1,000 outputs**

Team Lead ने 1,000 approve कर दिए।

QA:

**100 samples**

check करता है।

अगर:

**96 valid → 96% QA accuracy**

अगर:

**82 valid → 82%**

तो department को corrective action।

---

# Phase 6 — Days 61–75: Automation

अब repetitive workflows automate करेंगे।

Priority:

### High

- CRM updates
- Reporting
- Notifications
- Task creation
- Data movement
- Dashboard updates

### Medium

- Research assistance
- Content workflows
- QA sampling

### Low

- High-judgement decisions

**High-level decisions human-owned रहेंगे।**

---

# Phase 7 — Days 76–90: Optimization

अब सवाल होगा:

> “क्या system काम कर रहा है?”

Metrics:

- Revenue/FTE
- Cost/client
- Delivery margin
- QA defect rate
- Employee productivity
- Automation success
- Client retention
- Sales conversion

फिर:

**Keep → Improve → Automate → Outsource → Remove**

decision लिया जाएगा।

# Module 25 — Legal & Compliance SOP

**Owner:** COO  
**External support:** CA / CS / Lawyer  
**Internal coordinator:** Finance/HR designated owner

### Covers
- Contracts
- Employment documentation
- Client agreements
- Vendor agreements
- IP ownership
- Confidentiality/NDA
- Data/privacy obligations
- Tax/statutory compliance
- Regulatory notices

### Workflow

`Requirement → Review → Risk Assessment → External Legal Review → Approval → Signature → Storage → Renewal Tracking`

### KPI

| KPI | Target |
|---|---:|
| Contracts reviewed before signing | 100% |
| Expired contracts unnoticed | 0 |
| Critical compliance deadlines missed | 0 |
| Required documents archived | 100% |

**Rule:** कोई employee अपने authority से बाहर contract sign नहीं करेगा।

---

# Module 26 — Information Security & Incident Response

**Owner:** CTO  
**Control:** QA/COO

### Security layers

1. Identity & access
2. MFA
3. Least-privilege access
4. Device security
5. Data backup
6. Vendor access
7. Monitoring
8. Incident response

### Incident Levels

| Level | Example | Response |
|---|---|---|
| P1 | Major data/security breach | Immediate escalation |
| P2 | Significant system compromise | Urgent |
| P3 | Limited incident | Normal escalation |
| P4 | Minor issue | Routine |

### Incident workflow

`Detect → Contain → Assess → Escalate → Remediate → Verify → Document → Prevent recurrence`

### Mandatory incident record

- What happened?
- When?
- Systems affected?
- Data affected?
- Who discovered it?
- Immediate containment?
- Root cause?
- Corrective action?
- Final approval?

---

# Module 27 — Business Continuity & Backup

**Owner:** COO + CTO

हर critical process के लिए:

**Primary System + Backup Method + Owner + Recovery Procedure**

### Critical areas

- CRM
- Finance
- Client files
- Employee records
- Production systems
- Password/access management
- Communication

### Recovery priorities

| System | Priority |
|---|---|
| Client delivery systems | Critical |
| CRM | Critical |
| Finance | High |
| HR records | High |
| Internal documentation | Medium |

### Test

Backup होना पर्याप्त नहीं।

**Restore करके verify करना compulsory है।**

---

# Module 28 — Employee Grievance & Disciplinary SOP

**Owner:** HR  
**Escalation:** Department Head → COO

### Process

`Complaint → Acknowledgement → Investigation → Evidence → Discussion → Decision → Documentation → Follow-up`

### Principles

- Confidentiality
- No retaliation
- Evidence-based decision
- Consistent treatment
- Documentation

### Disciplinary levels

**Coaching → Written Warning → Final Warning → Separation**, जहाँ appropriate और legally permissible हो।

Serious misconduct में escalation सीधे higher level पर हो सकता है।

---

# Module 29 — Budgeting SOP

**Owner:** Finance Head  
**Approval:** COO/CEO

### Budget categories

- Payroll
- Software
- Marketing
- Sales
- Contractors
- Vendors
- Infrastructure
- Professional services
- Office/operations

### Budget cycle

`Forecast → Department Request → Finance Review → Management Approval → Allocation → Monthly Actual vs Budget → Variance Action`

### Dashboard

| Metric | Target |
|---|---|
| Budget variance | Within approved tolerance |
| Unapproved spending | 0 |
| Monthly financial close | On schedule |
| Cash forecast | Updated regularly |

---

# Module 30 — Customer Success Function

**Initial owner:** Delivery + Sales  
**Future owner:** Dedicated Customer Success Lead

### Customer lifecycle

`Sale → Handoff → Onboarding → Delivery → Health Check → Feedback → Renewal → Upsell`

### Customer Health Score

| Factor | Weight |
|---|---:|
| Delivery performance | 30% |
| Client satisfaction | 25% |
| Engagement | 15% |
| Results achieved | 20% |
| Commercial health | 10% |

### Status

🟢 Healthy  
🟡 At Risk  
🔴 Critical

### Rule

Client complaint को केवल “support issue” नहीं मानना।

उसे:

**Root Cause + Corrective Action + Prevention**

में convert करना है।

```text
Activity
 ↓

 ↓

 ↓
```

- Formula
- Source

होना चाहिए।

`Recognized/defined revenue according to accounting policy`

एक ही company में Sales, Finance और CEO के dashboards पर revenue की अलग-अलग definition नहीं होनी चाहिए।

सबसे आसान तरीका: **पूरी chat का Export Data करना जरूरी नहीं है।**

अगर option नहीं मिल रहा, तो:

1. इस chat को खोलो।
2. ऊपर **⋯ (three dots)** दबाओ।
3. **Share** या **Export** जैसा option देखो।
4. अगर **Copy** available है, तो conversation copy करके यहाँ paste कर दो।
5. अगर बहुत लंबी है, तो **कई हिस्सों में** भेज सकते हो—मैं उन्हें क्रम में जोड़ दूँगा।

**सबसे अच्छा तरीका:** अगर तुम्हारे पास conversation की `.txt`, `.html`, `.json` या `.md` file बन जाती है, तो सीधे यहाँ **upload** कर दो।

फिर मैं उसे **verbatim, बिना summary किए**, GitHub के `Public_Workspace` में डाल दूँगा।
